# Whelen Automotive Price List PL26.0WL — full reference

> Source: `Auto_List_PL26.0WL.pdf` (No. PL26.0WL, dated December 18, 2025; void June 30, 2026, extended Dec 15, 2026). Prices are USD list.

> Extracted verbatim (per-page, boilerplate stripped). Each price-list **printed page N** = **PDF page N+3** (printed p.1 starts on PDF page 4).

> Layout per entry in the source: a MODEL line, then one or more DESCRIPTION lines, then a `$price` line. Section/sub-section headers appear as plain prose lines with no price. `††`=NCNR special order, `†`=to be discontinued, `!`=specify length, `$`=lower price. Color-code wildcards: `*`=solo color, `#`=duo color, `**`/`####`=multiple slots.

## Quick index — products relevant to DTM Vehicle Builder

| Product family | Printed pages | PDF pages | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|

| Century™ Series Mini Lightbars | 36–37 | 39–40 | `whelen_century` (MC*) |

| Mini Liberty™ II | 37 | 40 | — |
| Responder® LP Series Mini Lightbars | 37–38 | 40–41 | `whelen_responder_lp` (R1LP*/R2LP*), fixed-config |
| Micro Freedom® (lightheads) | 14–15 | 17–18 | `whelen_freedom` (MCFLED2*) |
| Legacy® SOLO/DUO+/TRIO Series | 54–56 | 57–59 | `whelen_legacy` (EB*) |
| Liberty™ II Series | 56–59 | 59–62 | `whelen_liberty` (BB*/ITL*) |
| **Edge® 9X Series (9XS / 9XT) WeCanX** | **60–61** | **63–64** | `whelen_9x_edge` placeholder; made-to-order `9XS*/9XT*` |
| Century Elite™ Series | 61 | 64 | CE* — *candidate for the "CENATOR" placeholder?* |
| Options, Mounting Kits (Chevy/Dodge/Ford/Rivian/Perm) | 65–67 | 68–70 | Bar mount kits (MKEZ*, STPKT*/STPBK*) |
| Replacement Parts, Low-Profile Mounting & Strap Kits | 67–68 | 70–71 | Strap kits |

> **"CENATOR" is NOT a Whelen model** — it appears nowhere in PL26.0WL (no `cenat`/`senator` match). The `whelen_cenator` entry in `parts_db.json` is a placeholder with an invented SKU. Needs Seth to identify the real product (closest catalog name: **Century Elite™**, CE-series).
